# UAEU official source map

## Diploma and archive cautions

- No current standalone graduate diploma listing was found in the current official program indexes or College of Graduate Studies program list during this crawl. This is a discovery limitation, not proof that UAEU has never offered diplomas.
- Historical diploma evidence: [official accredited-programs document](https://www.uaeu.ac.ae/ar/about/pdf/uaeu_accredited_programs.pdf) and [legacy Curriculum and Instruction course catalog](https://webapps.uaeu.ac.ae/catalog/auto/department_ED_ED_CURR.asp). Historical courses and accreditation entries do not establish current admission availability.
- The archive hub exposes undergraduate, graduate and course catalogs from 2015–2016 through 2025–2026, with older program-year entries in the legacy Degree Index. Keep the year in the question and use the actual linked document; an archive link label can itself be inaccurate.
- This authoritative-only copy removes 55 nonauthoritative webtest/CMS discovery-link lines from the original crawl. The removed programme entry is not established as a current offering here; consult the genuine public programme index.

Prepared: 2026-11-03 (Asia/Dubai). Tester: Codex.

Scope: program links discovered from the official catalog indexes, current program listings, College of Graduate Studies listing, and official navigation hubs. Each program page was fetched before chatbot testing. Discovery is broad but is not a guarantee that hidden/unlinked or retired material has been found. Study-plan links are recorded where the official page exposes them; absence of a link is not proof that no plan exists. Current pages can disagree with dated cohort plans: retain program, year, entry term, and audience when evaluating an answer.

## Coverage and retrieval limitations

- Program links discovered: 172.
- Official pages fetched: 198; failed/non-200 fetches: 0.
- The live current listings include minors, micro-credentials, and accelerated options; these are retained and must not be mislabeled as full degree programs.
- Only https UAEU domains are accepted as official sources. Search snippets and the chatbot knowledge files are not ground truth.
- No chatbot question has been sent before completion of this discovery map.
